              def filtered_tickets
          
      191. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    191. 
            
              5
            

            

                @locations = Location.ordered
          
      192. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    192. 
            
              5
            

            

                @sort      = %w[id title].include?(params[:sort]) ? params[:sort] : "id"
          
      193. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    193. 
            
              5
            

            

                @direction = params[:direction] == "asc" ? "asc" : "desc"
          
      194. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    194. 
            

            

            
          
      195. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    195. 
            
              5
            

            

                tickets = Ticket.all.order(@sort => @direction).includes(:customer, :assignee, :location)
          
      196. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    196. 
            
              5
            

            

                tickets = tickets.where(ticket_type: params[:ticket_type])  if params[:ticket_type].present?
          
      197. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    197. 
            
              5
            

            

                tickets = tickets.where(assignee_id: nil)                  if params[:unassigned].present? || params[:assignment] == "unassigned"
          
      198. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    198. 
            
              5
            

            

                tickets = tickets.where(assignee_id: current_user.id)      if params[:assignment] == "self_assigned"
          
      199. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    199. 
            
              5
            

            

                tickets = tickets.where(assignee_id: params[:assignee_id]) if params[:assignee_id].present?
          
      200. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    200. 
            
              5
            

            

                tickets = tickets.where(location_id: params[:location_id]) if params[:location_id].present?
          
      201. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    201. 
            
              5
            

            

                if params[:search].present?
          
      202. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    202. 
            

            

                  search_term = params[:search].strip
          
      203. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    203. 
            

            

                  ticket_id   = search_term.delete_prefix("#")
          
      204. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    204. 
            

            

                  if ticket_id.match?(/\A\d+\z/)
          
      205. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    205. 
            

            

                    tickets = tickets.where(id: ticket_id)
          
      206. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    206. 
            

            

                  else
          
      207. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    207. 
            

            

                    term    = "%#{search_term}%"
          
      208. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    208. 
            

            

                    tickets = tickets.where("title ILIKE :term", term: term)
          
      209. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    209. 
            

            

                  end
          
      210.

              def cosine_similarity(a, b)

                  def termination_detail_attributes(fields)

                  def upsert_hiring_detail!(ticket, fields)

                  def upsert_termination_detail!(ticket, fields)

                    attrs = termination_detail_attributes(fields)

                  def infer_django_type(ticket, fields)
          
      159. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    159. 
            

            

                    case ticket.metadata["request_type"]
          
      160. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    160. 
            

            

                    when "Termination" then "Termination"
          
      161. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    161. 
            

            

                    when "New Hire"      then "Hiring"
          
      162. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    162. 
            

            

                    else
          
      163. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    163. 
            

            

                      status = field_lookup(fields, "STATUS").to_s.strip.downcase
          
      164. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    164. 
            

            

                      return "Termination" if %w[termination terminate departure].include?(status)
          
      165. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    165. 
            

            

                      return "Hiring"        if status == "hire"
          
      166. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    166. 
            

            

                      return "Termination"   if field_lookup(fields, "TERMINATION DATE").present?
          
      167. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    167. 
            

            

                      return "Termination"   if ticket.termination_detail.present?
          
      168. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    168. 
            

            

                      return "Hiring"        if ticket.hiring_detail.present?
          
      169. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    169. 
            

            

            
          
      170. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    170. 
            

            

                      "Hiring"
          
      171. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    171. 
            

            

                    end
          
      172. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    172. 
            

            

                  end
          
      173. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    173. 
            

            

            
          
      174. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    174. 
            

            

                  def field_lookup(fields, *keys)
          
      175. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    175. 
            

            

                    keys.each do |key|
          
      176. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    176. 
            

            

                      val = fields[key]
          
      177. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    177. 
            

            

                      return val if val.present?
          
      178. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    178. 
            

            

                    end
          
      179. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    179. 
            

            

                    nil
          
      180. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    180. 
            

            

                  end
          
      181. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    181. 
            

            

            
          
      182. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    182. 
            

            

                  def to_list(val)
          
      183. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    183. 
            

            

                    return [] if val.blank?
          
      184. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    184. 
            

            

            
          
      185. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    185. 
            

            

                    Array(val).flat_map { |item| item.to_s.split(",").map(&:strip) }.reject(&:blank?)
          
      186. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    186. 
            

            

                  end
          
      187. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    187. 
            

            

            
          
      188. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    188. 
            

            

                  def reject_none(values)
          
      189. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    189. 
            

            

                    to_list(values).reject { |v| v.casecmp?("none") || v.casecmp?("n/a") }
          
      190. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    190. 
            

            

                  end
          
      191. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    191. 
            

            

            
          
      192. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    192. 
            

            

                  def parse_date(val)
          
      193. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    193. 
            

            

                    return nil if val.blank?
          
      194. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    194. 
            

            

            
          
      195. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    195. 
            

            

                    Date.parse(val.to_s)
          
      196. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    196. 
            

            

                  rescue StandardError
          
      197. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    197. 
            

            

                    nil
          
      198. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    198. 
            

            

                  end
          
      199. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    199. 
            

            

            
          
      200. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    200. 
            

            

                  def yes_no?(val)
          
      201. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    201. 
            

            

                    val.to_s.strip.downcase.in?(%w[yes y true 1])
          
      202. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    202. 
            

            

                  end
          
      203. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    203. 
            

            

            
          
      204. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    204. 
            

            

                  def update_hiring_departure_ticket!(ticket, fields, request_type)
          
      205. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    205. 
            

            

                    full_name = field_lookup(fields, "FULLNAME")
          
      206. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    206. 
            

            

                    ticket.metadata = ticket.metadata.merge(
          
      207. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    207. 
            

            

                      "request_type" => request_type,
          
      208. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    208. 
            

            

                      "full_name"    => full_name
          
      209. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    209. 
            

            

                    )
          
      210. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    210. 
            

            

                    ticket.title = "#{request_type}: #{full_name}" if full_name.present?
          
      211. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    211. 
            

            

                    ticket.save!(validate: false)
          
      212. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    212. 
            

            

                  end
          
      213. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    213. 
            

            

            
          
      214. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    214. 
            

            

                  def hiring_or_termination(fields, django_ticket_type)
          
      215. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    215. 
            

            

                    status = field_lookup(fields, "STATUS").to_s.strip.downcase
          
      216. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    216. 
            

            

                    return :hire        if status == "hire"
          
      217. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    217. 
            

            

                    return :termination if %w[termination terminate departure].include?(status)
          
      218. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    218. 
            

            

                    return :termination if django_ticket_type == "Termination"
          
      219. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    219. 
            

            

                    return :hire        if django_ticket_type == "Hiring"
          
      220. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    220. 
            

            

                    return :hire        if field_lookup(fields, "FULLNAME").present? && field_lookup(fields, "TERMINATION DATE").blank?
          
      221. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    221. 
            

            

                    return :termination if field_lookup(fields, "TERMINATION REASON", "REASON", "TERMINATION DATE").present?
          
      222. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    222. 
            

            

            
          
      223. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    223. 
            

            

                    nil
          
      224. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    224. 
            

            

                  end
          
      225. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    225. 
            

            

                end
          
      226. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    226. 
            

            

              end
          
      227. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    227. 
            

            

            end
          
      228.
